windows.reflective_load.ReflectiveLoad
Executive Summary For management and non-technical stakeholders

What was found

1 hidden program was detected running inside this system's memory across 103 processes, loaded using a technique that bypasses normal Windows security monitoring.

Risk level

MEDIUM RISK

Findings

reflective_inj (Process ID 5332): a hidden code library (DLL) was found loaded into memory without registering with the operating system. It has no file on disk and is invisible to standard security tools.45/100 MEDIUM

What this means

This technique (MITRE ATT&CK T1620 — Reflective Code Loading) is used by advanced malware to operate entirely in memory, avoiding antivirus, endpoint detection, and file-based forensic tools. The hidden code is not registered with the Windows loader, making it invisible to standard system monitoring. Only memory forensics can detect it.

Recommended actions

  • Isolate the affected machine from the network
  • Preserve this memory capture — it is the primary forensic evidence
  • Escalate to the incident response team for further investigation
  • Search the SHA-256 hash(es) in threat intelligence platforms (VirusTotal, MISP)
  • Investigate how the affected process was started (parent process, command line)
  • Scan other machines in the environment for the same indicator
